## ===== briefs/ch34-t1-item7.md =====

# 12.2c tranche T1 — Item 7 (CH7-D1): one countdown block loop (`ClassNP/PolyTimeBlock{Loop,Tests,Majority}.lean`)

## Repository and branch — read this before anything else

- Clone `https://github.com/Shilun-Allan-Li/tcslib`; check out **`complexity/arora-barak-ch3-4`**,
  NOT `main`. Confirm `git merge-base --is-ancestor 1a0013d9750a6d760b6fd46b3c267fe18f08f4fa HEAD`; if it fails, **stop**.
- Branch off fresh (`refactor/12.2c-t1-blockloop`); record the base hash; never rebase.
- **Delivery by zip** (`12.2c-t1-blockloop.zip`): `REPORT.md`, the three full sources, a
  `git format-patch` series, a bundle, the sweep and axiom logs, the four screen outputs, and
  `SHA256SUMS`. Integration (no action for you): T1 (plan §4e), a side branch, a PR for the
  user, then a **separate audit gate**. The files are Aparna's (ch7); she agreed to this PR.

## What this is

A refactor, with no new mathematics and every public statement frozen. Read plan §4d item 7 and
§4e, `backlog.md` CH7-D1, the ledger's CH7-D1 row, `audits/ch7-fill-r2-findings.md` R2-2, and
`policy.md`.

- **The debt.** The OR, XOR-then-OR and majority loops re-prove one guard, countdown, length,
  orbit and emission lemma set. R2-2 puts Tests at **≥ 12/25**, Majority at **≥ 8/14** and Loop
  at ≥ 1/32. The auditor: it "can be shared while retaining separate OR/XOR/vote computations."
- **The design (binding).** The shared reasoning goes in `namespace Complexity.BlockLoop` in
  Loop (Task 1), plus an OR-flag engine in Tests (Task 2). Each loop keeps only its own
  content: the XOR test and mask, or the vote counters. The OR test becomes the XOR test with
  an all-`false` mask. Thin wrappers are deleted.

**Pre-ship check (maintainer, executed):** `audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item7PreShip.lean.txt`
(with its `.out`) runs the three refactored files verbatim (Loop L22–748, Tests L749–928,
Majority L929–1136) inside `namespace Complexity.Item7Probe`, beside the imported originals:
**0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 sorries**. It contains 26 `rfl` checks `@Item7Probe.X = @Complexity.X`,
2 checks of the redefined privates against their base bodies, and 72 axiom prints (26 base, 46
new), all within the triple, with the 26 pairs identical. `Item7PreShip-HomeContext.lean.txt`
runs the same bodies with only Loop's eight imports: 0/0/0.

**The probe is the reference implementation.** Land it with `Item7Probe` mapped to `Complexity`.
The full target files, headers and module docstrings included, are
`audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item7Ref-PolyTimeBlock{Loop,Tests,Majority}.lean.txt`. Shorten a proof
only if every check below still passes, and report each.

## Owned files (modify these and nothing else)

| File | Issue commit (lines, public / private) | Reference after |
|---|---|---|
| `ClassNP/PolyTimeBlockLoop.lean` | 500, 22 / 10 | 789, 39 / 11 |
| `ClassNP/PolyTimeBlockTests.lean` | 653, 3 / 22 | 228, 6 / 2 |
| `ClassNP/PolyTimeBlockMajority.lean` | 469, 1 / 13 | 243, 1 / 5 |

None has changed since `5ad155ee`. Net: −362 lines, −27 privates, +20 publics, all in
`Complexity.BlockLoop`, a namespace used nowhere else (repo and dependencies grepped).

## Task 1 — the generic layer (`PolyTimeBlockLoop.lean`)

After `polyTimeComputable_emitIter`, before `/-! ### Truncating bitwise XOR -/`, insert the
section `/-! ### The countdown block loop … -/` with `namespace BlockLoop`. It holds the 17
publics below, verbatim, and two private guards between `emit` and `polyTimeComputable_step`:
`polyTimeComputable_headNil : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD s)])` and
`polyTimeComputable_countdownNil`, the same at `pairFstD (pairSndD s)`
(`:= polyTimeComputable_headNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD`). Each `…` is at these
probe lines:
`polyTimeComputable_step` 327–340, `polyTimeComputable_emit` 343–348, 
`polyTimeComputable_countdownInit` 352–357, `step_done` 360–364, `length_step_le` 371–417, 
`length_iterate_le_of_slack` 423–442, `sliceTakeAt_pairEncode_drop` 446–449, 
`sliceDropAt_pairEncode_drop` 453–458, `orbit` 464–482, `iterate_done` 486–501, 
`lt_polyBudget_of_le` 504–507, `flatMap_emit_orbit` 513–529, `loop_output` 535–547, 
`polyTimeComputable_of_loop` 553–565, `polyTimeComputable_countdown` 571–588.
```lean
/-- One round of a countdown block loop: on `pairEncode h (pairEncode cd r)` with `h` and
`cd` nonempty it returns `pairEncode (upd h r) (pairEncode (cd.drop 1) (nxt h r))`; a state
whose head (done or malformed) or countdown is empty goes to the absorbing `blockDone`. -/
def step (upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then blockDone
  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then blockDone
  else
    pairEncode (upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
      (pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1) (nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
/-- The chunk function of a countdown block loop: `out s` exactly when the head of `s` is
nonempty and its countdown is empty, and `[]` otherwise. -/
def emit (out : List Bool → List Bool) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then []
  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then out s
  else []
/-- A countdown block loop's round `g = step upd nxt` is polynomial-time whenever its head
and remainder updates, read off the state, are (one `ite` on the disjoint guards). -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_step {g : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
    (hu : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))) :
    PolyTimeComputable g := …
/-- The chunk function `emit out` is polynomial-time whenever `out` is. -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_emit {out : List Bool → List Bool}
    (ho : PolyTimeComputable out) : PolyTimeComputable (emit out) := …
/-- The initial state `pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode cd (r z))`, with unary countdown `cd` of
length `a'·(|x z|+1)^k'`, is polynomial-time whenever `x` and `r` are. -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_countdownInit (h₀ : List Bool) {x r : List Bool → List Bool}
    (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r) (a' k' : ℕ) :
    PolyTimeComputable (fun z => pairEncode h₀
      (pairEncode (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))) := …
/-- `blockDone` is absorbing for every countdown block loop. -/
theorem step_done {g : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt) :
    g blockDone = blockDone := …
/-- One round of a countdown block loop respects the slack measure `|s| + c·|cd s|`
(`cd s = pairFstD (pairSndD s)`), up to `max _ 2`, provided every live update satisfies
`2·|upd h r| + |nxt h r| ≤ 2·|h| + |r| + 2 + c`.
**Proof sketch.** The done branches give `blockDone`; a live round on `⟨h, ⟨cd, r⟩⟩` drops
one countdown symbol, two symbols of encoding and `c` of slack, paying for the growth. -/
theorem length_step_le {g : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt) (c : ℕ)
    (hlive : ∀ h r, h ≠ [] →
      2 * (upd h r).length + (nxt h r).length ≤ 2 * h.length + r.length + 2 + c)
    (s : List Bool) :
    (g s).length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD (g s))).length ≤
      max (s.length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length) 2 := …
/-- An orbit envelope from a slack step bound: if every step of `g` respects
`|s| + c·|cd s| ≤ max (·) 2`, then every iterate of `g` from `w` has length at most
`b·(|w|+1)^1` whenever `2 ≤ b` and `c + 1 ≤ b`, the shape
`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter` consumes (induction, then `|cd w| ≤ |w|`). -/
theorem length_iterate_le_of_slack {g : List Bool → List Bool} (c b : ℕ)
    (hb : 2 ≤ b) (hcb : c + 1 ≤ b)
    (hg : ∀ s, (g s).length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD (g s))).length ≤
      max (s.length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length) 2)
    (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
    (g^[i] w).length ≤ b * (w.length + 1) ^ 1 := …
/-- Reading the `i`-th block: on `pairEncode x (u.drop (i·B))`, with `B = a·(|x|+1)^k`,
`sliceTakeAt a k` keeps `x` and the next `B` symbols. -/
theorem sliceTakeAt_pairEncode_drop (a k : ℕ) (x u : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
    sliceTakeAt a k (pairEncode x (u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)))) =
      pairEncode x ((u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k))).take (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)) := …
/-- Advancing one block: on `pairEncode x (u.drop (i·B))`, with `B = a·(|x|+1)^k`,
`sliceDropAt a k` returns `pairEncode x (u.drop ((i+1)·B))`. -/
theorem sliceDropAt_pairEncode_drop (a k : ℕ) (x u : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
    sliceDropAt a k (pairEncode x (u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)))) =
      pairEncode x (u.drop ((i + 1) * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k))) := …
/-- Closed form of a countdown block loop's orbit up to countdown exhaustion: from
`⟨P 0, ⟨1^K, Q 0⟩⟩`, after `i ≤ K` rounds the state is `⟨P i, ⟨1^(K-i), Q i⟩⟩`, given
nonempty heads and the one-round recurrences of the head and the remainder (induction
on `i`: both guards fail and the countdown loses one symbol). -/
theorem orbit {g : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K : ℕ} {s₀ : List Bool}
    (h₀ : s₀ = pairEncode (P 0) (pairEncode (List.replicate K true) (Q 0)))
    (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ [])
    (hupd : ∀ i < K, upd (P i) (Q i) = P (i + 1))
    (hnxt : ∀ i < K, nxt (P i) (Q i) = Q (i + 1))
    {i : ℕ} (hi : i ≤ K) :
    g^[i] s₀ = pairEncode (P i) (pairEncode (List.replicate (K - i) true) (Q i)) := …
/-- Once a countdown block loop reaches a live state with empty countdown, every later
iterate is `blockDone`: one more round hits the countdown guard (`step_done`). -/
theorem iterate_done {g : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
    {s₀ p q : List Bool} {K : ℕ} (hp : p ≠ [])
    (hK : g^[K] s₀ = pairEncode p (pairEncode [] q)) {i : ℕ} (hi : K < i) :
    g^[i] s₀ = blockDone := …
/-- Budget domination: `a'·(n+1)^k' < a'·(m+1)^k' + 1` whenever `n ≤ m`. -/
theorem lt_polyBudget_of_le (a' k' : ℕ) {n m : ℕ} (h : n ≤ m) :
    a' * (n + 1) ^ k' < a' * (m + 1) ^ k' + 1 := …
/-- Single emission: if the orbit of `g` from `s₀` is `⟨P i, ⟨1^(K-i), Q i⟩⟩` with nonempty
heads for `i ≤ K` and `blockDone` afterwards, then over any round budget `N > K` the
concatenated chunks of `emit out` are the one chunk `out ⟨P K, ⟨[], Q K⟩⟩`: only round `K`
emits (`Complexity.flatMap_range_eq_single`). -/
theorem flatMap_emit_orbit {g out : List Bool → List Bool} {s₀ : List Bool}
    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K N : ℕ}
    (horb : ∀ i ≤ K, g^[i] s₀ =
      pairEncode (P i) (pairEncode (List.replicate (K - i) true) (Q i)))
    (hdone : ∀ i, K < i → g^[i] s₀ = blockDone)
    (hKN : K < N) (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ []) :
    (List.range N).flatMap (fun i => emit out (g^[i] s₀)) =
      out (pairEncode (P K) (pairEncode [] (Q K))) := …
/-- The output of a countdown block loop from its recurrences: from
`s₀ = ⟨P 0, ⟨1^K, Q 0⟩⟩`, under the hypotheses of `orbit`, the concatenated chunks of
`emit out` over any round budget `N > K` are `out ⟨P K, ⟨[], Q K⟩⟩` (`orbit`,
`iterate_done`, `flatMap_emit_orbit`). -/
theorem loop_output {g out : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K N : ℕ} {s₀ : List Bool}
    (h₀ : s₀ = pairEncode (P 0) (pairEncode (List.replicate K true) (Q 0)))
    (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ [])
    (hupd : ∀ i < K, upd (P i) (Q i) = P (i + 1))
    (hnxt : ∀ i < K, nxt (P i) (Q i) = Q (i + 1)) (hKN : K < N) :
    (List.range N).flatMap (fun i => emit out (g^[i] s₀)) =
      out (pairEncode (P K) (pairEncode [] (Q K))) := …
/-- The headline assembly: if the round `g`, the chunk `e` and the initializer `init` are
polynomial-time, the orbit of `g` has a linear envelope, and the concatenated chunks of
`e` along the orbit from `init z`, over `a'·(|init z|+1)^k' + 1` rounds, are `f z`, then
`f` is polynomial-time (`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter` after `init`). -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_of_loop {g e init f : List Bool → List Bool}
    (hg : PolyTimeComputable g) (he : PolyTimeComputable e) (hinit : PolyTimeComputable init)
    (a' k' b : ℕ)
    (horbit : ∀ (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ), (g^[i] w).length ≤ b * (w.length + 1) ^ 1)
    (hf : ∀ z, (List.range (a' * ((init z).length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
      (fun i => e (g^[i] (init z))) = f z) :
    PolyTimeComputable f := …
/-- **A countdown block loop is polynomial-time.**  For `g = step upd nxt` and `e = emit out`
run from `⟨h₀, ⟨1^(a'(|x z|+1)^k'), r z⟩⟩`: if the updates, `out`, `x`, `r` are polynomial-time,
the live updates obey the slack bound `c`, and the chunks along the orbit are `f z`, then
`f` is polynomial-time (`polyTimeComputable_of_loop`, `length_iterate_le_of_slack`). -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_countdown {g e x r f : List Bool → List Bool}
    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} {out : List Bool → List Bool}
    (hg : g = step upd nxt) (he : e = emit out) (h₀ : List Bool) (a' k' c : ℕ)
    (hu : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
    (ho : PolyTimeComputable out) (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r)
    (hlive : ∀ h q, h ≠ [] →
      2 * (upd h q).length + (nxt h q).length ≤ 2 * h.length + q.length + 2 + c)
    (hf : ∀ z, (List.range (a' * ((pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode
        (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))).length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
      (fun i => e (g^[i] (pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode
        (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))))) = f z) :
    PolyTimeComputable f := …
```

## Task 2 — Tests

After `blockAt`, a second `namespace BlockLoop` holds three publics (probe lines:
`length_flag_le` 765–769, `any_range_succ_flag` 773–776, `polyTimeComputable_orLoop` 786–817):
```lean
/-- Replacing a nonempty word by the flag `[true]` does not lengthen it. -/
theorem length_flag_le {f : List Bool} (hf : f ≠ []) (b : Bool) :
    (if b then [true] else f).length ≤ f.length := …
/-- One OR round: raising the flag on the `i`-th test value extends the flag
`[(range i).any F]` to `[(range (i+1)).any F]`. -/
theorem any_range_succ_flag (F : ℕ → Bool) (i : ℕ) :
    (if F i then [true] else [(List.range i).any F]) = [(List.range (i + 1)).any F] := …
/-- **An OR-flag block loop computes the OR of its tests in polynomial time.**  Run from
`⟨[false], ⟨1^K z, r z⟩⟩`, `K z = a'·(|x z|+1)^k'`, the loop raising its flag when `t` holds
of the remainder and advancing the remainder by `nxt`.  If the remainders follow `Q z`
(`r z = Q z 0`, `nxt (Q z i) = Q z (i+1)`), `t (Q z i) = F z i`, `|nxt q| ≤ |q| + 2 + c`,
`|x z| ≤ |r z|`, and `t`, `nxt`, `x`, `r` are polynomial-time, then so is
`z ↦ [(range (K z)).any (F z)]`.
**Proof sketch.** `polyTimeComputable_countdown` with the flag update and `out = pairFstD`;
`loop_output` at heads `[(range i).any (F z)]`, by `any_range_succ_flag`. -/
theorem polyTimeComputable_orLoop {t : List Bool → Bool} {nxt x r : List Bool → List Bool}
    (Q : List Bool → ℕ → List Bool) {F : List Bool → ℕ → Bool} (a' k' c : ℕ)
    (ht : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [t (pairSndD (pairSndD s))]))
    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
    (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r)
    (hnlen : ∀ q, (nxt q).length ≤ q.length + 2 + c)
    (hxr : ∀ z, (x z).length ≤ (r z).length)
    (hQ0 : ∀ z, r z = Q z 0)
    (hadv : ∀ z i, i < a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k' → nxt (Q z i) = Q z (i + 1))
    (htest : ∀ z i, i < a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k' → t (Q z i) = F z i) :
    PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [(List.range (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k')).any (F z)]) := …
```
- **`polyTimeComputable_blockXorAnyTest`** is one `orLoop` call (L833–873): `t`, `nxt`, `Q` give the
  XOR test on `⟨v, ⟨x, u.drop (i·B)⟩⟩`, the carried mask and the block advance, at slack `c = 2`.
- **`polyTimeComputable_blockAnyTest`** comes after it (L912–925). It is a `convert` of the XOR
  test composed with `z ↦ ⟨z, xorD ⟨1^B, 1^B⟩⟩`, using two new privates, `zipWith_xor_self` and
  `zipWith_xor_blockAt` (L878–899).

## Ground rules (binding)

1. **Ownership.** The three files only; imports unchanged; escalate anything the spec cannot meet.
2. **Freeze.** Every base public keeps a byte-identical statement. Of the 26, 22 are
   byte-identical in full, including the six `def`s and `blockAt` (`Randomized/PolyTimeModel`
   unfolds `blockAt` with `simp`). `polyTimeComputable_xorD`, `_blockAnyTest`,

   paragraphs change.

   the *Main definitions* line.
4. **Privates.** Add no more than the reference (Loop +2, Tests +2). List every added or deleted
   declaration, and every changed private statement (`majStep`, `majEmit`). Moving
   `blockAnyTest` after `blockXorAnyTest` is sanctioned.

## Known pitfalls at this pin (from the probe)

- **Pass `(g := majStep V a k)` explicitly.** `hg := rfl` then recovers `upd`/`nxt` by
  δ-unfolding, which works only because `majStep` *is* `BlockLoop.step …`.
- **`rw [loop_output …]` cannot match through `def`s.** Use
  `have hout := loop_output (g := …) (s₀ := …) …; rw [hout]`, or `(…).trans ?_`.
- **λ-arguments arrive as β-redexes**: `simp only [BlockLoop.sliceTakeAt_pairEncode_drop]` reduces
  them, `rw` does not. **`isNil` exposes `decide (· = [])`**: `unfold step isNilB`, then case on the
  `decide`s. **`omega`** handles `max`, treats `c * m` as an atom: supply `c * (m+1) = c * m + c`.
- **Vote recurrence.** Rewrite the one-round count identity first, then the `show`
  subtraction; `if_neg` on `hcnt` leaves a `+ 0` that `Nat.add_zero` misses.
- **Screen magnets (measured).** Keep `polyTimeComputable_xorPairStep` as two `have`s and
  `polyTimeComputable_emit` with `hnone` and the guards. Their one-line forms pair cross-file
  with the headlines at 50–78%.

## 1. Duplication and the pre-screen

### Mechanical reproduction and limits

The pinned recursive Git tree contains **562 `TCSlib/**/*.lean` files**. The supplied bundle contains eight of them. Running the **unmodified** supplied script on those eight reproduces every line of the recorded result except the haystack-size label: **112 declarations, 3 exact-mode hits, 21 renamed-mode hits**, with identical partners, percentages, and shingle counts.

I then added the pinned `Configuration`, `Deterministic`, `Finite`, `Simulation`, `Build/Loop`, and `Build/Primitives` sources and reran it on fourteen files. The 24 reported hits remained identical. Thus the positive results are reproducible, but the absence of a reported hit does not exclude re-proved material. I did not independently replay the entire 562-file corpus; the supplied full-tree run remains evidence for that execution, not a proof of semantic nonduplication.

The surface total is

`18 EmitIterEmbed + 20 EmitIterBody + 32 BlockLoop + 25 Tests + 14 Majority + 3 PClosure headlines = 112`.

The “renamed” mode preserves keywords and punctuation but maps most ASCII-leading identifiers to the same token. It does not preserve which occurrences refer to the same identifier, and it does not abstract all Unicode identifiers. Its shingles are sets, not occurrence counts. The script reports a best partner, not every qualifying pair. These qualifications explain both false positives and omissions; they do not invalidate the reproduced output.

### Existing run facts reproduced in EmitIterEmbed

All source locations below are at `4502c30f`; line numbers refer to the actual Lean files, excluding bundle fences.

| EmitIterEmbed declaration | Existing result | Correspondence |
|---|---|---|
| `step_output_prefix`, lines 114–124 | `Build/Loop.lean`, private `FinTM.emLoop_step_prefix`, lines 4653–4667 | Identical configuration equality, after changing binder names and argument order: stepping with an existing output prefix equals prefixing the resulting configuration's output. |
| `runFrom_output_prefix`, lines 127–135 | `Build/Loop.lean`, private `FinTM.emLoop_run_prefix`, lines 4672–4680 | Identical run equality. The new proof inducts from the front of the run; the old proof inducts on the final step. That restructuring evades contiguous matching. |
| `runFrom_output_extends`, lines 138–155 | `Finite.lean`, `MultiTapeTM.output_prefix`, lines 105–117 | Specialize the existing theorem to times zero and `t`. A list-prefix witness is exactly an appended suffix, up to reversing the equality. The new declaration repeats the append-only induction. |
| `runFrom_of_halted`, lines 160–166 | `Deterministic.lean`, `MultiTapeTM.runFrom_of_halt`, lines 225–227 | The same statement specialized to Boolean symbols, with explicit/implicit arguments rearranged. The new declaration gives another induction instead of citing the existing theorem. |
| `state_isSome_of_runFrom`, lines 169–181 | `Build/Loop.lean`, private `FinTM.loop_live_prefix`, lines 183–192 | A live endpoint implies every state at an earlier or equal time is live. For `Option` states, “not `none`” is equivalent to “equals `some q` for some `q`.” The new proof repeats the same absorbing-halt contradiction. |

These five target declarations occupy **58 code lines** before docstrings. The three relevant private Loop declarations are also unchanged from the round-1 fork tree; they are not newly introduced by the merged-tree adaptation. The `Finite` blob is identical across those snapshots.

The public visibility of the new facts does not remove the debt. Exporting shared infrastructure can be useful, but independently re-proving an already established routine fact still falls within the pack's requested non-mechanical check. This finding concerns these five specific correspondences, not the proposition that every short adapter or every embedding construction is a copy.

### The acknowledged block-loop family

All six disclosed copy-side entries are confirmed. Their four Majority code spans are **15 + 16 + 46 + 8 = 85 lines**, so the displayed 4/14 copy-side calculation is correct. The two Tests entries occupy **16 + 30 = 46 code lines**; the screen table's 21-line entry for `xorStep_orbit_done` is not its 16-line declaration span.

However, the ledger's stated membership rule counts **either side** of a qualifying cross-file correspondence, once per file. The six disclosed pairs already give this source-inclusive union:

| File | Members contributed by the six disclosed pairs | Total |
|---|---|---:|
| `PolyTimeBlockMajority` | `length_majStep_iterate`, `majStep_orbit_done`, `length_majStep_le`, `polyTimeComputable_majInit` | **4/14** |
| `PolyTimeBlockTests` | `length_xorStep_iterate`, `anyStep_orbit_done`, `length_xorStep_le`, `polyTimeComputable_anyInit`, `xorStep_orbit_done`, `xorLoop_output`, `anyLoop_output` | **7/25** |

The later-copy-only convention is explicitly stated in the Chapter-7 pre-screen, but it is different from the standing ledger convention. Both numbers can be reported under distinct labels; **2/25 cannot stand in for the cumulative source-inclusive Tests total**. This is why R2-2 invokes the amended rule's further-file threshold exception, rather than treating every accounting correction as major.

The screen also misses substantial cross-file adaptations within the same loop family. For reproducible witnesses, I stripped comments and whitespace from proof bodies, consistently replaced the corresponding `anyStep`/`majStep`, `anyEmit`/`majEmit`, `anyInit`/`majInit`, and associated helper names by shared names, and found disjoint matching blocks of at least 25 characters. These are lower bounds on reproduced source text, not the published shingle scores:

| Tests source → Majority counterpart | Shared source-proof characters | Reproduced argument |
|---|---:|---|
| `polyTimeComputable_anyStep` → `polyTimeComputable_majStep` | 1056/1157 = **91.3%** | The same guard, countdown, payload, and conditional-composition assembly, with additional vote operations. |
| `polyTimeComputable_anyEmit` → `polyTimeComputable_majEmit` | 379/406 = **93.3%** | The same two guarded emission branches, with a vote-comparison computation inserted. |
| `anyStep_orbit` → `majStep_orbit` | 749/926 = **80.9%** | The countdown/slicing induction and block advance recur; the vote-counter arithmetic is additional material. |
| `anyLoop_output` → `majLoop_output` | 795/853 = **93.2%** | The same budget domination and before/at/after-exhaustion argument using `flatMap_range_eq_single`; majority adds the count comparison. |

These four additional target members produce **at least 8/14 = 57.1% in Majority**. Three of their sources are new to the seven-member Tests union (`anyLoop_output` was already included), yielding **at least 10/25 = 40% in Tests**. Even excluding the two disclosed in-file copy-side entries, the eight cross-file source members exhibited here alone give **8/25 = 32%** in Tests. Thus the crossing does not depend on how the in-file exception is applied.

Two further length correspondences also merit recording. `Tests.length_anyStep_le` contributes 953/1370 = **69.6%** of its normalized proof to `Majority.length_majStep_le`, adding another source-side member. `Tests.length_anyStep_iterate` has exactly the same proof body as `BlockLoop.length_xorPairStep_iterate` after substituting `(anyStep V a k) → xorPairStep` and `length_anyStep_le V a k → length_xorPairStep_le`; it adds another Tests member and one BlockLoop member. The confirmed totals therefore reach **at least 12/25 in Tests, 8/14 in Majority, and 1/32 in BlockLoop**. These are lower bounds, not an assertion of an exhaustive replacement census. The threshold-changing discrepancy already follows from the earlier, smaller inventories; the additional evidence belongs in the same reconciliation.

The named generic-lemma resolution remains plausible: the repeated guard, countdown, orbit, and single-emission reasoning can be shared while retaining separate OR/XOR/vote computations. I found no reason here to declare that resolution impossible.

### Class B and the borderline cases

| Candidate | Verdict |

| `polyTimeComputable_take1` / `polyTimeComputable_tail` | Accept the mirror classification: distinct take/drop operations, proved by the corresponding existing primitives. |
| `polyTimeComputable_sliceTakeAt` / `polyTimeComputable_sliceDropAt` | Accept the mirror classification: the same scheduling interface applied to the complementary take/drop operations. |

Their proof bodies are exactly equal after the explicit substitutions `sliceDropAt → xorPairStep`, `z → s`, `p → a`, `u → b`; their full declarations differ in parameters and operation. Under the ledger's explicit convention for in-file near-duplicates, record it without adding a census member. It is more precise to disclose this than to describe it merely as a coincidental proof shape. |
| `majStep` / `polyTimeComputable_majStep` | Exclude this definition-versus-proof match. The proof restates the definition's case expression to establish its complexity; this pair is not two copies of proved material. The separate cross-file proof adaptation listed above is a different comparison. |

## 2. EmitIter versus Build/Embed: contract facts

| Aspect | `EmitIterEmbed` | `Build/Embed` |
|---|---|---|
| State transport | `embedCfg` maps `SS → B`; **no injectivity hypothesis** is imposed despite the parameter name `inject`. `embed_step` can start at an arbitrary host state `b` with the required transition. `padAction` itself accepts an arbitrary function on optional states. | The closed transformers preserve the state type. The returning transformers use the specific tags `Sum.inl` and `Sum.inr ()`. These APIs do not quantify over an arbitrary host state map or arbitrary matching host transition table. |
| Tape placement | The first `m` of `k` tapes, using `Fin.castLE` and `m ≤ k`. | Any genuine injection `ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k`, with source tape coordinates preserved. This is whole-tape relocation, not multiple virtual tapes packed onto one physical tape. |
| Unselected tapes | `embedCfg` initializes them blank, heads zero. `padAction` leaves them untouched, but the supplied run theorem starts from that blank-padding configuration. | Explicit arbitrary frame tapes and head positions are carried through the run; frame theorems state their preservation. In the silent flavor, the designated capture tape has its specified buffer contents instead. |
| Output | Actions forward the source emission. Configuration transport preserves source output. Separate public theorems state output-prefix commutation and append-only extension. | Forwarding transport has output `pre ++ c.output`. Silent transport captures that word on `cap`, keeps physical output `out₀`, and requires `cap` outside the selected bank in its public run contracts. Both execute a final halting transition's emission. |
| Simulation premise | `embed_run` compares an arbitrary host and module, requiring matching transitions away from `ex` and live, non-`ex` source states at every `j < t`. The endpoint may halt or reach `ex`. | Closed transformers supply their own transition tables; their lockstep theorems hold for all durations, including halted tails, with no avoidance or liveness guard. |
| Avoidance / return | `SafeRun` means endpoint equality plus avoidance for **all `j < t`**, including zero. It has zero, prepend, and concatenation laws. It requires neither a live endpoint nor an avoided endpoint. A positive anchor-to-anchor round is therefore not a `SafeRun` avoiding its own starting anchor. | Returning contracts assume a live start, liveness before `T`, and halt at `T`; they conclude live return at time `T`, with no earlier return-anchor visit. They execute through the source halt. The public contracts exclude an initially halted zero-time handover. No general `SafeRun` composition API is stated here. |
| Space | No explicit visited-set or per-tape-space contracts in this module. | Selected-tape visited-set equality, stationary frame sets, capture-space bound, and returning/closed visited-set comparisons are exported. |

In particular, source avoidance alone does not imply avoidance of `inject ex` in an arbitrary host: distinct source states may share their image. The actual body uses disjoint constructors and proves the needed anchor exclusion. This qualifies the word “injection” without reopening the already accepted forward-simulation theorem.

The supplied `Embed.lean` has filled proofs, notwithstanding stale “statement skeleton / all sorried” module prose. The comparison above follows its declarations, not that historical status description.

## 3. Transfer and comment-sweep verification

All eight bundled Lean files have Git blob hashes equal to their entries in the pinned merged tree. Comparing the nine round-1 files with `0c22ae33` confirms the claimed six byte-identical blobs. For `EmitIterEmbed`, `PolyTimeBlockLoop`, and `PClosure`, I fetched the old contents and independently confirmed equality after removing nested block comments, line comments, and whitespace. The old SHA-256 prefixes match the attestation: `de94b02751582f84`, `5654f6f1b1b12025`, and `5481969b35302efd`.

The attached diff reverses cleanly against the bundled final files. Its reconstructed preimage Git blobs begin `424ec64c` (EmitIterEmbed), `611d5e11` (BlockLoop), and `0370605d` (PClosure), exactly as its index lines specify. Every changed character lies in a block comment. The additional fork-to-pre-sweep PClosure differences are precisely the two default-projection docstring corrections described by the transfer attestation.

- The XOR bullet correctly credits the emit-iteration construction used by `polyTimeComputable_xorD`.
- The EmitIterEmbed summary correctly removes the two nonexistent public exports and identifies the private body helpers. Only the older pack inventory remains stale (R2-4).
- The new PClosure paragraph accurately describes default decoding and re-encoded queries. It does not impose malformed-input rejection.

For the last point, the reductions are explicit:

1. If the outer pair decoding fails, both default components are `[]`. The first-component length is zero, so the scheduled query count is `a' * (0 + 1)^k' = a'`. Every ordinary block is empty.
2. In the nested headline, outer failure also makes the inner components and the mask empty. If the outer pair is valid but its inner word is malformed, the two inner components are empty while the outer mask is retained. In either case every queried block is empty, and `List.zipWith xor mask [] = []`.
3. Consequently, in each malformed case just described, every query is exactly `pairEncode [] []`. Each of the three headline languages accepts exactly when **`0 < a'` and `pairEncode [] [] ∈ V`**: OR needs one query, and strict majority of a constant answer accepts precisely under the same condition. With zero queries all three reject. The retained outer mask has no effect after XOR truncation against an empty block.

This establishes the advertised totalization, including the nested case, directly from the displayed statements and `blockAt`. No newly added comment misstates that behavior.

The seven-module recheck log records seven zero exits. The carried merged sweep records 23 successful checks and the disclosed concentration admission; the axiom log has 36 entries, with `sorryAx` only for that disclosed result. These execution logs, and the reported lint result, were not independently regenerated. They do not resolve the two duplication-governance majors above.

## ===== audits/12.2c-agent-reports/t1-blockloop-REPORT.md =====

# 12.2c T1 item 7 — CH7-D1 block-loop refactor

Completed Tasks 1–4 by landing the three reference files verbatim. All required checks pass. **new copies: none**.

## Provenance and scope

- Repository: `https://github.com/Shilun-Allan-Li/tcslib`; source branch: `complexity/arora-barak-ch3-4`.
- Base: `199644473a34b6da24862d94ae1eece7f8138005`. `git merge-base --is-ancestor 1a0013d9750a6d760b6fd46b3c267fe18f08f4fa HEAD` passed before branching.
- Delivery branch: `refactor/12.2c-t1-blockloop`; commit: `6008127069b310c104c3f27c1b21137683d8bf7c`. No rebase, push or PR.
- The owned sources were unchanged since `5ad155ee` (empty `unchanged-since-issue.diff`). Only `TCSlib/Complexity/ClassNP/PolyTimeBlock{Loop,Tests,Majority}.lean` changed in the commit. Imports are byte-identical.
- Read the entire binding brief and policy, plan §4d item 7 and §4e, backlog CH7-D1, the ledger row, R2-2, and all three reference sources. No implementation deviates from the probe; no proof was shortened.
- Escalations: **none**. Item 6, `Build/EmitIter*`, `PClosure`, and all other source files remain outside this change.

## Tasks and size changes

1. Added the shared countdown layer in `Complexity.BlockLoop` in Loop: 17 publics and two private guards, directly after the emit-iteration combinator.
2. Added the three-public OR-flag engine in Tests. XOR-then-OR supplies only its test, mask and remainder operations. The plain OR test composes the XOR test with an all-false mask; its old loop and wrappers are deleted.
3. Majority uses the shared countdown step, emission and output contracts, retaining the vote-specific computation. The two private definitions are definitionally equal to their old bodies.
4. The XOR transducer uses `polyTimeComputable_of_loop` and the common orbit-length bound. Its private iterate-length lemma is deleted, and its step-computability proof has the reference two-`have` form.

| File | Lines before → after (delta) | Public before → after | Private before → after |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `PolyTimeBlockLoop.lean` | 500 → 789 (+289) | 22 → 39 | 10 → 11 |
| `PolyTimeBlockTests.lean` | 653 → 228 (-425) | 3 → 6 | 22 → 2 |
| `PolyTimeBlockMajority.lean` | 469 → 243 (-226) | 1 → 1 | 13 → 5 |
| Total | 1,622 → 1,260 (−362) | 26 → 46 (+20) | 45 → 18 (−27) |

## Statement freeze and declaration inventory

All 26 base public statement substrings (including whitespace and binder order) are byte-identical. Of these, 22 full declaration bodies and their docstrings are byte-identical, including all six base definitions and `blockAt`. The other four retain the same prose before the **Proof sketch** paragraph. Full final-file SHA-256 hashes match the supplied reference files.

The four changed public proofs are `polyTimeComputable_xorD`, `polyTimeComputable_blockAnyTest`, `polyTimeComputable_blockXorAnyTest`, and `polyTimeComputable_blockMajorityTest`. The OR theorem moves after the XOR theorem as sanctioned.

### Changed existing private declarations

`polyTimeComputable_xorPairStep` and `majLoop_output` retain byte-identical statements and receive exactly the reference proofs. All other surviving private declarations are unchanged except the following sanctioned redefinitions.

**Complexity.majEmit**

Before:
```lean
private def majEmit (s : List Bool) : List Bool
```
After:
```lean
private def majEmit : List Bool → List Bool
```

**Complexity.majStep**

Before:
```lean
private noncomputable def majStep (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ) (s : List Bool) : List Bool
```
After:
```lean
private noncomputable def majStep (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ) : List Bool → List Bool
```

`majStep` is now `BlockLoop.step (fun _ _ => [true])` with the vote-push remainder update. `majEmit` is `BlockLoop.emit` with the vote comparison. Both have the same elaborated type and value as before; the supplied two `rfl` checks were replayed successfully.

## Duplication governance

Unmodified `audits/evidence/retrofit/copy-text-screen.py` was run on the baseline and final source trees. Its four required raw outputs are included. Counts concern declarations whose extracted bodies meet the screen’s length cutoff, not all declarations.

| Screen | Declarations before → after | Pairs before → after | Reproduced characters before → after |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Six files | 104 → 94 | 125 → 41 | 47,734 → 10,045 |
| Owned three | 60 → 50 | 102 → 18 | 41,608 → 3,919 |

The six-file cross-file pair count falls from 65 to 2. Both remaining pairs pre-existed and are outside CH7-D1: `PClosure::union_mem_P ← PolyTimeBlockLoop::polyTimeComputable_or` (68%) and `← polyTimeComputable_not` (52%). Tests and Majority occur in no final pair.

The three-file result consists of Loop’s 17 old in-file pairs and the sole new reported in-file near-duplicate: `BlockLoop.polyTimeComputable_step ← polyTimeComputable_xorPairStep`, 87/148 characters (59%), arising from the shared `drop 1` vocabulary. This is the exact disclosed reference pair. No new cross-file pair exists; no new in-file pair reaches 90%. Loop’s pre-existing in-file pairs, including `length_xorPairStep_le` / `length_sliceDropAt_le`, are recorded out of scope.

| File | R2-2 confirmed family before → after | Owned-three screen cross-file source/target union before → after |
|---|---:|---:|
| Tests | ≥12/25 → 0/8 | 22/25 → 0/8 |
| Majority | ≥8/14 → 0/6 | 12/14 → 0/6 |
| Loop | ≥1/32 → 0/50 | 6/32 → 0/50 |

The census table uses the owned-three screen, as in the brief’s 6/32 → 0/50 Loop row. Across all six files the Loop union is instead 8/32 → 2/50, because its two Boolean helpers participate in the explicitly excluded, pre-existing PClosure pairs. The R2-2 source-inclusive family vanishes because the duplicated guards, countdown, length, orbit, initializer and single-emission proofs are replaced by shared citations, and the obsolete OR/XOR/majority wrappers are deleted. The shared generic layer is counted once; Loop’s disclosed in-file convention does not add family members.

For the rename rerun, the three files were copied into temporary trees, and the Majority copy was processed with `sed -e 's/maj/any/g' -e 's/blockMajorityTest/blockAnyTest/g'` before the same three-file screen. Raw before and after outputs are included:

```text
declarations screened: 60; copy pairs (target reproduces >= half of source): 103; reproduced text: 42,567 chars
declarations screened: 50; copy pairs (target reproduces >= half of source): 18; reproduced text: 3,919 chars
```

**new copies: none**. The screen is a textual heuristic; the explicit declaration inventory and reference-conformity checks also establish the requested family collapse.

## Verification

- Lean 4.25.0; mathlib `029db123ddaa7f8fd0d18cea3b1b33bf84dacd1e`; dependency pins verified.
- Ran `lake exe cache get` on the 72 direct Mathlib imports in the supplied closure; successful retry unpacked 2,783 cache files. No `lake build` was run.
- All Lean proof checks used the unmodified `scripts/lean_check_tree.sh`, which removes the previous output and requires a fresh `.olean` and no errors.
- Baseline bootstrap: **218/218 modules**, 0 errors, 1 existing sorry warning, from `briefs/orders/ch34-t1-blockloop.txt`.
- Final replay: **24/24 modules**, 0 errors, 0 sorry warnings. Owned files: **0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 sorries**. Every downstream sorry-warning line is byte-identical to the corresponding baseline line.
- Replayed the supplied pre-ship probe against the baseline: **26 public `rfl` checks + 2 private-body `rfl` checks**, 72 axiom prints, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 sorries.
- Printed axioms independently for the 26 baseline publics and all 46 final publics. The first 26 final print entries are byte-identical to the complete baseline output. All 46 use only `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`, with no `sorryAx`.
- `git diff --check` passed; only the three owned tracked files changed.

Lint result:
```text
style_lint: 0 FAIL, 5 WARN over 19 files
```

The five lint warnings are size warnings in untouched `EXP`, `Nondeterminism`, `SAT`, `TMSAT`, and `Tautology`. The three owned files have no lint warnings; Loop’s 789-line size is the sanctioned reference size and is reported at INFO.

The 24-module replay order is included as `replay-order.txt`. It is exactly the reverse import closure of the three owned files within the supplied 218-module order, including all specified facades and 11 Karp–Lipton/Meyer modules.

Final sweep tail:

Environment repairs were limited to local verification infrastructure. The sandbox PID namespace initially prevented Lean from locating its executable; the included `proc_self_compat.c` maps only the current process’s `/proc/<pid>/exe` query to `/proc/self/exe`. No Lean binary or kernel was modified. The first cache attempts failed from that path issue and disk pressure; the successful retry is logged. The working repository and fresh `.olean` tree used the available memory-backed filesystem. No verification gap remains.

## Package

The ZIP contains this report; three full final sources; a one-commit `git format-patch` series; an incremental Git bundle requiring the recorded base; baseline/final sweep and axiom logs; the four required screen outputs and two rename outputs; lint, freeze and scope evidence; reproduction scripts; and `SHA256SUMS`. The bundle and patch have the same commit and touch only the three owned files.

Apply the patch on the recorded base, or fetch `refactor/12.2c-t1-blockloop` from the bundle in a repository containing that base. Integration and the separate audit gate remain the maintainer’s workflow.

+/-! ### The countdown block loop
+
+The OR, XOR-then-OR and strict-majority block tests (`PolyTimeBlockTests`,
+`PolyTimeBlockMajority`) run one loop shape on the state word
+`pairEncode h (pairEncode cd r)`: a head `h` (flag or live marker), a unary countdown
+`cd`, and a remainder `r` carrying the scanned pair and any accumulated data.  The
+declarations below prove that shape's polynomial-time, length, orbit and emission facts
+once, for arbitrary head and remainder updates; each block test supplies only its own
+update, per-round test and output. -/
+
+namespace BlockLoop
+
+/-- One round of a countdown block loop: on `pairEncode h (pairEncode cd r)` with `h` and
+`cd` nonempty it returns `pairEncode (upd h r) (pairEncode (cd.drop 1) (nxt h r))`; a state
+whose head (done or malformed) or countdown is empty goes to the absorbing `blockDone`. -/
+def step (upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then blockDone
+  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then blockDone
+  else
+    pairEncode (upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
+      (pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1) (nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
+
+/-- The chunk function of a countdown block loop: `out s` exactly when the head of `s` is
+nonempty and its countdown is empty, and `[]` otherwise. -/
+def emit (out : List Bool → List Bool) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then []
+  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then out s
+  else []
+
+/-- The head guard of a countdown block loop is polynomial-time. -/
+private theorem polyTimeComputable_headNil :
+    PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD s)]) :=
+  polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+
+/-- The countdown guard of a countdown block loop is polynomial-time. -/
+private theorem polyTimeComputable_countdownNil :
+    PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s))]) :=
+  polyTimeComputable_headNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+
+/-- A countdown block loop's round `g = step upd nxt` is polynomial-time whenever its head
+and remainder updates, read off the state, are (one `ite` on the disjoint guards). -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_step {g : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
+    (hu : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
+    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))) :
+    PolyTimeComputable g := by
+  subst hg
+  have hcd : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => pairFstD (pairSndD s)) :=
+    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hdone := polyTimeComputable_or polyTimeComputable_headNil polyTimeComputable_countdownNil
+  convert polyTimeComputable_ite hdone (polyTimeComputable_const blockDone)
+    (hu.pairEncode ((polyTimeComputable_tail.comp hcd).pairEncode hn)) using 1
+  funext s
+  unfold step isNilB
+  cases decide (pairFstD s = []) <;> cases decide (pairFstD (pairSndD s) = []) <;> rfl
+
+/-- The chunk function `emit out` is polynomial-time whenever `out` is. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_emit {out : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (ho : PolyTimeComputable out) : PolyTimeComputable (emit out) := by
+  have hnone : PolyTimeComputable (fun _ : List Bool => ([] : List Bool)) :=
+    polyTimeComputable_const []
+  exact polyTimeComputable_ite polyTimeComputable_headNil hnone
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite polyTimeComputable_countdownNil ho hnone)
+
+/-- The initial state `pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode cd (r z))`, with unary countdown `cd` of
+length `a'·(|x z|+1)^k'`, is polynomial-time whenever `x` and `r` are. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_countdownInit (h₀ : List Bool) {x r : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r) (a' k' : ℕ) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (fun z => pairEncode h₀
+      (pairEncode (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))) :=
+  (polyTimeComputable_const h₀).pairEncode
+    (((polyTimeComputable_polyUnary a' k').comp hx).pairEncode hr)
+
+/-- `blockDone` is absorbing for every countdown block loop. -/
+theorem step_done {g : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt) :
+    g blockDone = blockDone := by
+  subst hg
+  simp [step, blockDone, isNilB]
+
+/-- One round of a countdown block loop respects the slack measure `|s| + c·|cd s|`
+(`cd s = pairFstD (pairSndD s)`), up to `max _ 2`, provided every live update satisfies
+`2·|upd h r| + |nxt h r| ≤ 2·|h| + |r| + 2 + c`.
+**Proof sketch.** The done branches give `blockDone`; a live round on `⟨h, ⟨cd, r⟩⟩` drops
+one countdown symbol, two symbols of encoding and `c` of slack, paying for the growth. -/
+theorem length_step_le {g : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt) (c : ℕ)
+    (hlive : ∀ h r, h ≠ [] →
+      2 * (upd h r).length + (nxt h r).length ≤ 2 * h.length + r.length + 2 + c)
+    (s : List Bool) :
+    (g s).length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD (g s))).length ≤
+      max (s.length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length) 2 := by
+  subst hg
+  unfold step
+  cases h1 : isNilB (pairFstD s) with
+  | true =>
+    simp only [↓reduceIte]
+    refine le_trans ?_ (le_max_right _ _)
+    simp [blockDone, length_pairEncode, pairFstD_nil]
+  | false =>
+    simp only [Bool.false_eq_true, ↓reduceIte]
+    cases h2 : isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) with
+    | true =>
+      simp only [↓reduceIte]
+      refine le_trans ?_ (le_max_right _ _)
+      simp [blockDone, length_pairEncode, pairFstD_nil]
+    | false =>
+      simp only [Bool.false_eq_true, ↓reduceIte]
+      have hf : pairFstD s ≠ [] := by simpa [isNilB] using h1
+      have hcd : pairFstD (pairSndD s) ≠ [] := by simpa [isNilB] using h2
+      have hs := eq_pairEncode_of_pairFstD_ne hf
+      have ht := eq_pairEncode_of_pairFstD_ne hcd
+      refine le_trans ?_ (le_max_left _ _)
+      have hlens : s.length =
+          2 * (pairFstD s).length + 2 +
+            (2 * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length + 2 + (pairSndD (pairSndD s)).length) := by
+        conv_lhs => rw [hs]
+        rw [length_pairEncode]
+        congr 2
+        conv_lhs => rw [ht]
+        rw [length_pairEncode]
+      have hcd1 : 1 ≤ (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length :=
+        Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (by simpa [List.length_eq_zero_iff] using hcd)
+      have hc : c * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length =
+          c * ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length - 1) + c := by
+        obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ : ∃ m, (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length = m + 1 :=
+          ⟨_, (Nat.sub_add_cancel hcd1).symm⟩
+        rw [hm, Nat.add_sub_cancel]
+        ring
+      have hl := hlive (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s)) hf
+      simp only [pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode, length_pairEncode, List.length_drop]
+      omega
+
+/-- An orbit envelope from a slack step bound: if every step of `g` respects
+`|s| + c·|cd s| ≤ max (·) 2`, then every iterate of `g` from `w` has length at most
+`b·(|w|+1)^1` whenever `2 ≤ b` and `c + 1 ≤ b`, the shape
+`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter` consumes (induction, then `|cd w| ≤ |w|`). -/
+theorem length_iterate_le_of_slack {g : List Bool → List Bool} (c b : ℕ)
+    (hb : 2 ≤ b) (hcb : c + 1 ≤ b)
+    (hg : ∀ s, (g s).length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD (g s))).length ≤
+      max (s.length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD s)).length) 2)
+    (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    (g^[i] w).length ≤ b * (w.length + 1) ^ 1 := by
+  have hmax : (g^[i] w).length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD (g^[i] w))).length ≤
+      max (w.length + c * (pairFstD (pairSndD w)).length) 2 := by
+    induction i with
+    | zero => simp
+    | succ i ih =>
+      rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply']
+      exact le_trans (hg _) (max_le ih (le_max_right _ _))
+  have h1 : (pairFstD (pairSndD w)).length ≤ w.length :=
+    le_trans (length_pairFstD_le _) (length_pairSndD_le w)
+  have h2 : c * (pairFstD (pairSndD w)).length ≤ c * w.length := Nat.mul_le_mul_left c h1
+  have h3 : (c + 1) * w.length ≤ b * w.length := Nat.mul_le_mul_right _ hcb
+  have h4 : (c + 1) * w.length = c * w.length + w.length := by ring
+  rw [pow_one, Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_one]
+  omega
+
+/-- Reading the `i`-th block: on `pairEncode x (u.drop (i·B))`, with `B = a·(|x|+1)^k`,
+`sliceTakeAt a k` keeps `x` and the next `B` symbols. -/
+theorem sliceTakeAt_pairEncode_drop (a k : ℕ) (x u : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    sliceTakeAt a k (pairEncode x (u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)))) =
+      pairEncode x ((u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k))).take (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)) := by
+  rw [sliceTakeAt, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- Advancing one block: on `pairEncode x (u.drop (i·B))`, with `B = a·(|x|+1)^k`,
+`sliceDropAt a k` returns `pairEncode x (u.drop ((i+1)·B))`. -/
+theorem sliceDropAt_pairEncode_drop (a k : ℕ) (x u : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    sliceDropAt a k (pairEncode x (u.drop (i * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k)))) =
+      pairEncode x (u.drop ((i + 1) * (a * (x.length + 1) ^ k))) := by
+  rw [sliceDropAt, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, List.drop_drop]
+  congr 2
+  ring
+
+/-- Closed form of a countdown block loop's orbit up to countdown exhaustion: from
+`⟨P 0, ⟨1^K, Q 0⟩⟩`, after `i ≤ K` rounds the state is `⟨P i, ⟨1^(K-i), Q i⟩⟩`, given
+nonempty heads and the one-round recurrences of the head and the remainder (induction
+on `i`: both guards fail and the countdown loses one symbol). -/
+theorem orbit {g : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
+    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K : ℕ} {s₀ : List Bool}
+    (h₀ : s₀ = pairEncode (P 0) (pairEncode (List.replicate K true) (Q 0)))
+    (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ [])
+    (hupd : ∀ i < K, upd (P i) (Q i) = P (i + 1))
+    (hnxt : ∀ i < K, nxt (P i) (Q i) = Q (i + 1))
+    {i : ℕ} (hi : i ≤ K) :
+    g^[i] s₀ = pairEncode (P i) (pairEncode (List.replicate (K - i) true) (Q i)) := by
+  subst hg h₀
+  induction i with
+  | zero => simp
+  | succ i ih =>
+    have hii : i < K := hi
+    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih (Nat.le_of_lt hii)]
+    have hcd : K - i = (K - (i + 1)) + 1 := by omega
+    have hPi : P i ≠ [] := hP i (Nat.le_of_lt hii)
+    rw [hcd, List.replicate_succ]
+    simp [step, isNilB, hPi, hupd i hii, hnxt i hii]
+
+/-- Once a countdown block loop reaches a live state with empty countdown, every later
+iterate is `blockDone`: one more round hits the countdown guard (`step_done`). -/
+theorem iterate_done {g : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
+    {s₀ p q : List Bool} {K : ℕ} (hp : p ≠ [])
+    (hK : g^[K] s₀ = pairEncode p (pairEncode [] q)) {i : ℕ} (hi : K < i) :
+    g^[i] s₀ = blockDone := by
+  subst hg
+  obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ : ∃ j, i = j + (K + 1) := ⟨i - (K + 1), by omega⟩
+  rw [Function.iterate_add_apply]
+  have hend : (step upd nxt)^[K + 1] s₀ = blockDone := by
+    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', hK]
+    simp [step, isNilB, hp]
+  rw [hend]
+  clear hi
+  induction j with
+  | zero => simp
+  | succ j ih => rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih, step_done rfl]
+
+/-- Budget domination: `a'·(n+1)^k' < a'·(m+1)^k' + 1` whenever `n ≤ m`. -/
+theorem lt_polyBudget_of_le (a' k' : ℕ) {n m : ℕ} (h : n ≤ m) :
+    a' * (n + 1) ^ k' < a' * (m + 1) ^ k' + 1 := by
+  have := Nat.mul_le_mul_left a' (Nat.pow_le_pow_left (Nat.add_le_add_right h 1) k')
+  omega
+
+/-- Single emission: if the orbit of `g` from `s₀` is `⟨P i, ⟨1^(K-i), Q i⟩⟩` with nonempty
+heads for `i ≤ K` and `blockDone` afterwards, then over any round budget `N > K` the
+concatenated chunks of `emit out` are the one chunk `out ⟨P K, ⟨[], Q K⟩⟩`: only round `K`
+emits (`Complexity.flatMap_range_eq_single`). -/
+theorem flatMap_emit_orbit {g out : List Bool → List Bool} {s₀ : List Bool}
+    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K N : ℕ}
+    (horb : ∀ i ≤ K, g^[i] s₀ =
+      pairEncode (P i) (pairEncode (List.replicate (K - i) true) (Q i)))
+    (hdone : ∀ i, K < i → g^[i] s₀ = blockDone)
+    (hKN : K < N) (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ []) :
+    (List.range N).flatMap (fun i => emit out (g^[i] s₀)) =
+      out (pairEncode (P K) (pairEncode [] (Q K))) := by
+  refine flatMap_range_eq_single hKN (fun i _ => ?_)
+  rcases Nat.lt_trichotomy i K with hiK | hiK | hiK
+  · rw [if_neg (by omega), horb i (le_of_lt hiK)]
+    have hne : K - i ≠ 0 := by omega
+    simp [emit, isNilB, hP i (le_of_lt hiK), hne]
+  · rw [if_pos hiK, hiK, horb K le_rfl, Nat.sub_self, List.replicate_zero]
+    simp [emit, isNilB, hP K le_rfl]
+  · rw [if_neg (by omega), hdone i hiK]
+    simp [emit, blockDone, isNilB]
+
+/-- The output of a countdown block loop from its recurrences: from
+`s₀ = ⟨P 0, ⟨1^K, Q 0⟩⟩`, under the hypotheses of `orbit`, the concatenated chunks of
+`emit out` over any round budget `N > K` are `out ⟨P K, ⟨[], Q K⟩⟩` (`orbit`,
+`iterate_done`, `flatMap_emit_orbit`). -/
+theorem loop_output {g out : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} (hg : g = step upd nxt)
+    (P Q : ℕ → List Bool) {K N : ℕ} {s₀ : List Bool}
+    (h₀ : s₀ = pairEncode (P 0) (pairEncode (List.replicate K true) (Q 0)))
+    (hP : ∀ i ≤ K, P i ≠ [])
+    (hupd : ∀ i < K, upd (P i) (Q i) = P (i + 1))
+    (hnxt : ∀ i < K, nxt (P i) (Q i) = Q (i + 1)) (hKN : K < N) :
+    (List.range N).flatMap (fun i => emit out (g^[i] s₀)) =
+      out (pairEncode (P K) (pairEncode [] (Q K))) := by
+  have horb := fun i (hi : i ≤ K) => orbit hg P Q h₀ hP hupd hnxt hi
+  have hK := horb K le_rfl
+  rw [Nat.sub_self, List.replicate_zero] at hK
+  exact flatMap_emit_orbit P Q horb (fun _ hi => iterate_done hg (hP K le_rfl) hK hi) hKN hP
+
+/-- The headline assembly: if the round `g`, the chunk `e` and the initializer `init` are
+polynomial-time, the orbit of `g` has a linear envelope, and the concatenated chunks of
+`e` along the orbit from `init z`, over `a'·(|init z|+1)^k' + 1` rounds, are `f z`, then
+`f` is polynomial-time (`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter` after `init`). -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_of_loop {g e init f : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hg : PolyTimeComputable g) (he : PolyTimeComputable e) (hinit : PolyTimeComputable init)
+    (a' k' b : ℕ)
+    (horbit : ∀ (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ), (g^[i] w).length ≤ b * (w.length + 1) ^ 1)
+    (hf : ∀ z, (List.range (a' * ((init z).length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
+      (fun i => e (g^[i] (init z))) = f z) :
+    PolyTimeComputable f := by
+  have heq : f = (fun w => (List.range (a' * (w.length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
+      (fun i => e (g^[i] w))) ∘ init := by
+    funext z
+    exact (hf z).symm
+  rw [heq]
+  exact (polyTimeComputable_emitIter hg he a' k' b 1 horbit).comp hinit
+
+/-- **A countdown block loop is polynomial-time.**  For `g = step upd nxt` and `e = emit out`
+run from `⟨h₀, ⟨1^(a'(|x z|+1)^k'), r z⟩⟩`: if the updates, `out`, `x`, `r` are polynomial-time,
+the live updates obey the slack bound `c`, and the chunks along the orbit are `f z`, then
+`f` is polynomial-time (`polyTimeComputable_of_loop`, `length_iterate_le_of_slack`). -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_countdown {g e x r f : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {upd nxt : List Bool → List Bool → List Bool} {out : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hg : g = step upd nxt) (he : e = emit out) (h₀ : List Bool) (a' k' c : ℕ)
+    (hu : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => upd (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
+    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairFstD s) (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
+    (ho : PolyTimeComputable out) (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r)
+    (hlive : ∀ h q, h ≠ [] →
+      2 * (upd h q).length + (nxt h q).length ≤ 2 * h.length + q.length + 2 + c)
+    (hf : ∀ z, (List.range (a' * ((pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode
+        (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))).length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
+      (fun i => e (g^[i] (pairEncode h₀ (pairEncode
+        (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))))) = f z) :
+    PolyTimeComputable f := by
+  subst he
+  exact polyTimeComputable_of_loop (polyTimeComputable_step hg hu hn)
+    (polyTimeComputable_emit ho) (polyTimeComputable_countdownInit h₀ hx hr a' k') a' k'
+    (c + 2) (length_iterate_le_of_slack c (c + 2) (by omega) (by omega)
+      (length_step_le hg c hlive)) hf
+
+end BlockLoop
+

-/-! #### The OR-aggregating loop -/
-
-/-- One round of the OR-aggregating block loop on state
-`pairEncode [flag] (pairEncode countdown (pairEncode x rem))`: OR the
-indicator of the current block into the flag, drop the block, decrement the
-unary countdown.  A done or malformed state is sent to the absorbing
-`blockDone`; an exhausted countdown is sent to `blockDone` one step after the
-chunk function has emitted the flag. -/
-private noncomputable def anyStep (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
-  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then blockDone
-  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then blockDone
-  else
-    pairEncode
-      (if MultiTapeTM.indicator V (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD s))) then [true]
-        else pairFstD s)
-      (pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1)
-        (sliceDropAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
-
-/-- The chunk function: emit the flag exactly at countdown exhaustion. -/
-private def anyEmit (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
-  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then []
-  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then pairFstD s
-  else []
-
-/-- The initial state: clear flag, unary countdown `a'·(n+1)^k'`, and the
-normalized input pair. -/

-  pairEncode [false]
-    (pairEncode (List.replicate (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k') true)
-      (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (pairSndD z)))
-

-  have hzz : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => pairSndD (pairSndD s)) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD

-    polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
-  have ht2 : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s))]) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp hcd

-      (fun s => [MultiTapeTM.indicator V (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))]) :=

-      pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1)

-/-- One loop round never grows the state beyond `max` with the done state's

-      have hslice' : (sliceDropAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD s))).length ≤
-          (pairSndD (pairSndD s)).length + 2 :=
-        le_trans hslice (by omega)
-      omega

-/-- Orbit envelope for the OR loop, in the shape
-`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter` consumes. -/
-private theorem length_anyStep_iterate (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ)
-    (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
-    ((anyStep V a k)^[i] w).length ≤ 2 * (w.length + 1) ^ 1 := by
-  have hmax : ((anyStep V a k)^[i] w).length ≤ max w.length 2 := by

-    | zero => simpa using le_max_left _ _

-      rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply']

-/-- The done state is absorbing. -/
-private theorem anyStep_done (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ) :
-    anyStep V a k blockDone = blockDone := by
-  unfold anyStep

-/-- Closed form of the OR loop's orbit up to countdown exhaustion.
-**Proof sketch.** Induction on the round index: the guards see a one-bit flag
-and a positive countdown, so the step fires; the slice primitives advance the
-remainder by one block, `List.range_succ` extends the OR by the current
-block's indicator, and the countdown loses one `true`. -/
-private theorem anyStep_orbit (V : Language Bool) (a k a' k' : ℕ) (z : List Bool)
-    {i : ℕ} (hi : i ≤ a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k') :
-    (anyStep V a k)^[i] (anyInit a' k' z) =
-      pairEncode
-        [(List.range i).any (fun j => MultiTapeTM.indicator V
-          (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z j)))]
-        (pairEncode
-          (List.replicate (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' - i) true)
-          (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
-            ((pairSndD z).drop (i * (a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k))))) := by
-  induction i with
-  | zero => simp [anyInit]
-  | succ i ih =>
-    have hii : i ≤ a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' := Nat.le_of_succ_le hi
-    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih hii]
-    have hcd : a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' - i =
-        (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' - (i + 1)) + 1 := by omega
-    rw [hcd, List.replicate_succ]
-    unfold anyStep
-    rw [if_neg (by simp [isNilB]), pairSndD_pairEncode, if_neg (by simp [isNilB])]
-    rw [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
-    congr 1
-    · rw [sliceTakeAt, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
-      rw [List.range_succ, List.any_append]
-      cases hb : MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
-          (((pairSndD z).drop (i * (a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k))).take
-            (a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k))) with
-      | true =>
-        have hb' : MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z i))
-            = true := hb
-        simp [hb']
-      | false =>
-        have hb' : MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z i))
-            = false := hb
-        simp [hb']
-    · congr 1
-      · rw [pairFstD_pairEncode]
-        simp
-      · rw [sliceDropAt, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, List.drop_drop]
-        congr 2
-        ring
-
-/-- Beyond exhaustion the orbit sits at the done state. -/
-private theorem anyStep_orbit_done (V : Language Bool) (a k a' k' : ℕ) (z : List Bool)
-    {i : ℕ} (hi : a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' < i) :
-    (anyStep V a k)^[i] (anyInit a' k' z) = blockDone := by
-  set K := a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' with hK
-  obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ : ∃ j, i = j + (K + 1) := ⟨i - (K + 1), by omega⟩
-  rw [Function.iterate_add_apply]
-  have hend : (anyStep V a k)^[K + 1] (anyInit a' k' z) = blockDone := by
-    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', anyStep_orbit V a k a' k' z (le_refl K)]
-    unfold anyStep
-    rw [if_neg (by simp [isNilB]), pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode]
-    rw [if_pos (by simp [isNilB, hK])]
-  rw [hend]
-  clear hi
-  induction j with
-  | zero => simp
-  | succ j ih => rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih, anyStep_done]

-/-- The OR loop's machine-level output is the single aggregated bit.
-**Proof sketch.** The countdown exhausts within the machine's larger round
-budget (the input embeds its own first component, and the schedule is
-monotone).  Rounds before exhaustion emit nothing, the exhaustion round emits
-the flag, and the absorbing done state emits nothing after it, so the
-single-live-chunk concatenation lemma applies. -/
-private theorem anyLoop_output (V : Language Bool) (a k a' k' : ℕ) (z : List Bool) :
-    (List.range (a' * ((anyInit a' k' z).length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
-      (fun i => anyEmit ((anyStep V a k)^[i] (anyInit a' k' z))) =
-      [(List.range (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k')).any
-        (fun j => MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z j)))] := by
-  set K := a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k' with hK
-  have hlen : (pairFstD z).length ≤ (anyInit a' k' z).length := by
-    rw [anyInit, length_pairEncode, length_pairEncode, length_pairEncode]
-    omega
-  have hKN : K < a' * ((anyInit a' k' z).length + 1) ^ k' + 1 := by
-    have := Nat.mul_le_mul_left a'
-      (Nat.pow_le_pow_left (Nat.add_le_add_right hlen 1) k')
+/-! #### The OR-flag block loop -/
+
+namespace BlockLoop
+
+/-- Replacing a nonempty word by the flag `[true]` does not lengthen it. -/
+theorem length_flag_le {f : List Bool} (hf : f ≠ []) (b : Bool) :
+    (if b then [true] else f).length ≤ f.length := by
+  cases b
+  · simp
+  · simpa using List.length_pos_of_ne_nil hf
+
+/-- One OR round: raising the flag on the `i`-th test value extends the flag
+`[(range i).any F]` to `[(range (i+1)).any F]`. -/
+theorem any_range_succ_flag (F : ℕ → Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    (if F i then [true] else [(List.range i).any F]) = [(List.range (i + 1)).any F] := by
+  rw [List.range_succ, List.any_append]
+  cases h : F i <;> simp [h]
+
+/-- **An OR-flag block loop computes the OR of its tests in polynomial time.**  Run from
+`⟨[false], ⟨1^K z, r z⟩⟩`, `K z = a'·(|x z|+1)^k'`, the loop raising its flag when `t` holds
+of the remainder and advancing the remainder by `nxt`.  If the remainders follow `Q z`
+(`r z = Q z 0`, `nxt (Q z i) = Q z (i+1)`), `t (Q z i) = F z i`, `|nxt q| ≤ |q| + 2 + c`,
+`|x z| ≤ |r z|`, and `t`, `nxt`, `x`, `r` are polynomial-time, then so is
+`z ↦ [(range (K z)).any (F z)]`.
+**Proof sketch.** `polyTimeComputable_countdown` with the flag update and `out = pairFstD`;
+`loop_output` at heads `[(range i).any (F z)]`, by `any_range_succ_flag`. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_orLoop {t : List Bool → Bool} {nxt x r : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (Q : List Bool → ℕ → List Bool) {F : List Bool → ℕ → Bool} (a' k' c : ℕ)
+    (ht : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [t (pairSndD (pairSndD s))]))
+    (hn : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => nxt (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
+    (hx : PolyTimeComputable x) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r)
+    (hnlen : ∀ q, (nxt q).length ≤ q.length + 2 + c)
+    (hxr : ∀ z, (x z).length ≤ (r z).length)
+    (hQ0 : ∀ z, r z = Q z 0)
+    (hadv : ∀ z i, i < a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k' → nxt (Q z i) = Q z (i + 1))
+    (htest : ∀ z i, i < a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k' → t (Q z i) = F z i) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [(List.range (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k')).any (F z)]) := by
+  refine polyTimeComputable_countdown
+    (g := step (fun h q => if t q then [true] else h) (fun _ q => nxt q)) (out := pairFstD)
+    rfl rfl [false] a' k' c (polyTimeComputable_ite ht (polyTimeComputable_const [true])
+      polyTimeComputable_pairFstD) hn polyTimeComputable_pairFstD hx hr
+    (fun h q hh => by
+      have := length_flag_le hh (t q)
+      have := hnlen q
+      omega) (fun z => ?_)
+  have hlen : (x z).length ≤ (pairEncode [false] (pairEncode
+      (List.replicate (a' * ((x z).length + 1) ^ k') true) (r z))).length := by
+    have := hxr z
+    simp only [length_pairEncode]
     omega
-  refine flatMap_range_eq_single hKN (fun i _ => ?_)
-  rcases Nat.lt_trichotomy i K with hiK | rfl | hiK
-  · rw [if_neg (by omega), anyStep_orbit V a k a' k' z (le_of_lt hiK)]
-    unfold anyEmit
-    rw [if_neg (by simp [isNilB]), pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode]
-    rw [if_neg (by simp [isNilB]; omega)]
-  · rw [if_pos rfl, anyStep_orbit V a k a' k' z (le_refl K)]
-    unfold anyEmit
-    rw [if_neg (by simp [isNilB]), pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode]
-    rw [if_pos (by simp [isNilB]; omega), pairFstD_pairEncode]
-  · rw [if_neg (by omega), anyStep_orbit_done V a k a' k' z hiK]
-    unfold anyEmit
-    rw [if_pos (by simp [blockDone, isNilB])]

-/-- The OR-aggregated block test of a polynomial-time one-bit indicator is
-polynomial-time: one bit saying whether some of the `a'·(n+1)^k'` blocks of
-length `a·(n+1)^k` passes the test on `Turing.pairEncode`d (first component,
-block).
-
-**Proof sketch.** An instance of `Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter`.
-The loop state is `pairEncode [flag] (pairEncode countdown (pairEncode x rem))`
-with a unary countdown initialized at `a'·(|x|+1)^k'`
-(`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_polyUnary`); each round ORs the indicator of
-`sliceTakeAt a k` into the flag, drops the block (`sliceDropAt a k`), and
-decrements; the chunk function emits `[flag]` exactly at countdown exhaustion
-(the step then moves to an absorbing done state), so the concatenated output
-is the single aggregated bit.  The orbit is computed in closed form by
-induction on the round index. -/
-theorem polyTimeComputable_blockAnyTest {V : Language Bool}
-    (hV : PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [MultiTapeTM.indicator V z]))
-    (a k a' k' : ℕ) :
-    PolyTimeComputable (fun z =>
-      [(List.range (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k')).any
-        (fun i => MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z i)))]) := by
-  have hloop := polyTimeComputable_emitIter (polyTimeComputable_anyStep hV a k)
-    polyTimeComputable_anyEmit a' k' 2 1 (length_anyStep_iterate V a k)
-  have heq : (fun z => [(List.range (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k')).any
-      (fun i => MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z i)))]) =
-      ((fun w => (List.range (a' * (w.length + 1) ^ k' + 1)).flatMap
-        (fun i => anyEmit ((anyStep V a k)^[i] w))) ∘ anyInit a' k') := by
-    funext z
-    rw [Function.comp_apply, anyLoop_output V a k a' k' z]
-  rw [heq]
-  exact hloop.comp (polyTimeComputable_anyInit a' k')
-
-/-! #### The XOR-shifted OR loop -/
-
-/-- One round of the XOR-shifted OR loop on state
-`pairEncode [flag] (pairEncode countdown (pairEncode v (pairEncode x u)))`:
-XOR the mask `v` with the current block of `u`, test paired with `x`, OR
-into the flag, drop the block, decrement. -/
-private noncomputable def xorStep (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ) (s : List Bool) : List Bool :=
-  if isNilB (pairFstD s) then blockDone
-  else if isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s)) then blockDone
-  else
-    pairEncode
-      (if MultiTapeTM.indicator V
-          (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
-            (xorD (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
-              (pairSndD (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))))))) then [true]
-        else pairFstD s)
-      (pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1)
-        (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
-          (sliceDropAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))))
-
-/-- The initial state of the XOR-shifted loop on the nested pair
-`⟨⟨x, u⟩, v⟩`: clear flag, unary countdown `a'·(|x|+1)^k'`, the mask `v`, and
-the normalized `⟨x, u⟩`. -/
-private def xorInit (a' k' : ℕ) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
-  pairEncode [false]
-    (pairEncode
-      (List.replicate (a' * ((pairFstD (pairFstD w)).length + 1) ^ k') true)
-      (pairEncode (pairSndD w)
-        (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD w)) (pairSndD (pairFstD w)))))
-
-/-- The XOR-shifted round is polynomial-time.
-**Proof sketch.** As the OR round, with the per-round test precomposed with
-the truncating XOR of the carried mask and the sliced block
-(`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_xorD`). -/
-private theorem polyTimeComputable_xorStep {V : Language Bool}
-    (hV : PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [MultiTapeTM.indicator V z])) (a k : ℕ) :
-    PolyTimeComputable (xorStep V a k) := by
-  have hcd : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => pairFstD (pairSndD s)) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
-  have hmask : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
-      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
-  have hzz : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
-      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
-  have ht1 : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD s)]) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
-  have ht2 : PolyTimeComputable (fun s => [isNilB (pairFstD (pairSndD s))]) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp hcd
-  have hblk : PolyTimeComputable (fun s =>
-      pairSndD (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp ((polyTimeComputable_sliceTakeAt a k).comp hzz)
-  have htest : PolyTimeComputable (fun s =>
-      [MultiTapeTM.indicator V
-        (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
-          (xorD (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
-            (pairSndD (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))))))]) :=
-    hV.comp ((polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hzz).pairEncode
-      (polyTimeComputable_xorD.comp (hmask.pairEncode hblk)))
-  have hflag : PolyTimeComputable (fun s =>
-      if MultiTapeTM.indicator V
-          (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s))))
-            (xorD (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
-              (pairSndD (sliceTakeAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))))))) then [true]
-        else pairFstD s) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_ite htest (polyTimeComputable_const [true])
-      polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
-  have hrest : PolyTimeComputable (fun s =>
-      pairEncode ((pairFstD (pairSndD s)).drop 1)
-        (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))
-          (sliceDropAt a k (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD s)))))) :=
-    (polyTimeComputable_tail.comp hcd).pairEncode
-      (hmask.pairEncode ((polyTimeComputable_sliceDropAt a k).comp hzz))
-  have hinner := polyTimeComputable_ite ht2 (polyTimeComputable_const blockDone)
-    (hflag.pairEncode hrest)
-  exact polyTimeComputable_ite ht1 (polyTimeComputable_const blockDone) hinner
-
-private theorem polyTimeComputable_xorInit (a' k' : ℕ) :
-    PolyTimeComputable (xorInit a' k') := by
-  have hx : PolyTimeComputable (fun w => pairFstD (pairFstD w)) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
-  have hu : PolyTimeComputable (fun w => pairSndD (pairFstD w)) :=
-    polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD

-    (polyTimeComputable_polyUnary a' k').comp hx
-  exact (polyTimeComputable_const [false]).pairEncode
-    (hcnt.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.pairEncode (hx.pairEncode hu)))

-/-- Orbit envelope for the XOR-shifted loop. -/
-private theorem length_xorStep_iterate (V : Language Bool) (a k : ℕ)
-    (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :

-/-- Closed form of the XOR-shifted loop's orbit up to countdown exhaustion.
-**Proof sketch.** As the OR orbit, with the carried mask constant and the

+/-! #### The OR test as the XOR test with a zero mask -/
+
+/-- XOR of a word with itself is the all-`false` word of its length. -/
+private theorem zipWith_xor_self (l : List Bool) :
+    List.zipWith xor l l = List.replicate l.length false := by
+  induction l with
+  | nil => rfl
+  | cons b l _ => simp [List.replicate_succ]
+
+/-- XOR with an all-`false` mask of the block length leaves a block unchanged. -/
+private theorem zipWith_xor_blockAt (a k : ℕ) (z : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    List.zipWith xor (List.replicate (a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k) false)
+      (blockAt a k z i) = blockAt a k z i := by
+  have h : (blockAt a k z i).length ≤ a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k :=
+    List.length_take_le _ _
+  generalize blockAt a k z i = l at h ⊢
+  generalize a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k = n at h ⊢
+  induction l generalizing n with
+  | nil => simp
+  | cons b l ih =>
+    cases n with
+    | zero => simp at h
+    | succ n =>
+      rw [List.replicate_succ, List.zipWith_cons_cons, ih n (by simpa using h)]
+      simp
+
+/-- The OR-aggregated block test of a polynomial-time one-bit indicator is
+polynomial-time: one bit saying whether some of the `a'·(n+1)^k'` blocks of
+length `a·(n+1)^k` passes the test on `Turing.pairEncode`d (first component,
+block).
+
+**Proof sketch.** The XOR-then-OR test with an all-`false` mask of the block length:
+on `⟨z, 0^B⟩`, where `B = a·(|x|+1)^k`, the XOR-shifted blocks are the blocks of `z`
+themselves (each has length at most `B`), so this test is
+`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_blockXorAnyTest` composed with `z ↦ ⟨z, 0^B⟩`.  The mask
+`0^B` is computed as `xorD ⟨1^B, 1^B⟩` (`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_polyUnary`,
+`Complexity.polyTimeComputable_xorD`). -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_blockAnyTest {V : Language Bool}
+    (hV : PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [MultiTapeTM.indicator V z]))
+    (a k a' k' : ℕ) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (fun z =>
+      [(List.range (a' * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k')).any
+        (fun i => MultiTapeTM.indicator V (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (blockAt a k z i)))]) := by
+  have hU : PolyTimeComputable
+      (fun z => List.replicate (a * ((pairFstD z).length + 1) ^ k) true) :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_polyUnary a k).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hpad := polyTimeComputable_id.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_xorD.comp (hU.pairEncode hU))
+  convert (polyTimeComputable_blockXorAnyTest hV a k a' k').comp hpad using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [Function.comp, id, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, xorD_pairEncode,
+    zipWith_xor_self, List.length_replicate, zipWith_xor_blockAt]
 
 end Complexity
